Potential algorithms:
A. Multiplicative weights on LSE with FFT gradient. Better than gradient. We can implement 
- p length n, sum 1.
- beta anneal.
- Compute b = conv(p,p) via FFT.
- w = softmax(beta*(b-bmax)).
- v = convolution/gradient vector = 2 cross? (p length n) such that ∂F/∂p_i = v_i.
- Update p_new = p * exp(-eta * (v - mean(v?)))? Since p on simplex, update log p? Multiplicative: log p_{new} = log p - eta(v - λ); choose λ = mean(log? maybe logarithmic update). Simpler: p_{new}=p*exp(-eta*(v - mean(v))); normalize. This is mirror descent with subgradient, preserves positive. Step size can be small. It may converge slowly; can anneal eta.
- Actually for objective F≈max b, subgradient w active. Multiplicative update is like exponentiating negative gradient. For large n, effective step maybe 1/n? Need tune.

Algorithm for p update:
- Compute b, w.
- Compute g = gradient vector (∂F_β/∂p) (not scaled).
- Center g: dg = g - mean(g) (since under sum fixed).
- Normalize dg by scale=max(abs(dg)) or standard deviation to unit scale.
- p *= exp(-step * dg/scale); normalize.
- This update direction depends only on relative gradient; step e.g. 0.05-0.5. It is a multiplicative mirror descent with gradient scaling. Since p_i positive. May be slow but manageable for thousands iterations. We can anneal step: smaller near optimum, or use acceptance of exact max.
- To avoid degenerating to zeros, maintain floor by adding epsilon after update? p_i can underflow; can renormalize and maybe clip p_i = max(p_i, floor) then renormalize; floor ~1e-8/n? But zeros allowed. Clipping floor helps.
- Use line search: try step in [0.1,0.05,0.025,...] accept if Fβ or exact max decreases? Multiplicative with normalized direction can overshoot; use backtracking.

Alternatively, use projected gradient on p: p_new = normalize(p + step*(?)). But p can negative.

Let's derive update mathematically for softmax + normalized gradient:
Given p=softmax θ. Let J = LSEβ(b). Let E = ∂J/∂p = 2C_r? The gradient on θ is Γ_i = p_i (E_i - m), where m=∑pE. We can replace step direction D_i = (E_i - mean(E))/scale, where mean(E) approximate m? Since m weighted, not unweighted; But for p roughly uniform, mean≈m. For nonuniform, projection tangent to sum constraint on p is E - λ where λ=∑pE. For multiplicative log update, the correct negative gradient in log space is E_i - λ? Let's derive objective gradient under p with sum=1: tangent direction δp with ∑δp=0. The Riemannian gradient is E - λ1 with λ=∑pE. For mirror descent, update log p = log p - η(E - λ). So use g_i = E_i - λ, not unweighted mean. λ = ∑p_i E_i. We can compute λ. For J scaled, okay. Normalize gf=(E - λ)/scale. Then p *= exp(-step*gf_norm) (or θ update). This is a valid mirror descent step in p (not softmax standard but equivalent up to p normalization). Use p update may be faster than θ (no exp of θ accumulation). But p can underflow; clip/floor and renorm.

We can incorporate step size with line search using exact F (maxb) or smooth J. Use scaled objective J=n*maxb maybe. For line search, compute b after update and exact max. Use smooth J for gradient.

Algorithm optimize_p(n, init, deadline):
- p normalized positive. We'll use arrays float64.
- FFT size: for convolution length L=2n-1, size1=pow2(L). For gradient conv rev_p and w length L, size2=pow2(n+L-1=3n-2). Precompute rev? p reversed each iter. Could compute r=p[::-1] copy.
- beta: tune from low to high. beta controls weight. Initial maybe beta = 50 * n? Since b scale 1/n. We can set beta = 200.0 * n? In _lse, argument beta*(b-m). If b spread ~1/n, beta*n? Let's compute: If beta=1e5 for n=1000, β*b ~100 for differences comparable 1/n. Use beta = 1000. Maybe dynamic: beta = max(1.0, beta_target / maxb) where beta_target maybe 20 to 200. The previous used beta_target/maxb = target* n, so 12*n. We can use beta = target_beta / maxb (target_beta=30 to 300). This yields effective β*(maxb - second) around target_beta for gap. Since maxb~0.75/n, beta=40/(0.75/n)≈53n. Good. We'll animate beta_target from 10 to 500.
- w = softmax(beta*(b - maxb)). If beta huge, w concentrates on top. To avoid too sparse, cap beta at e.g. 1e6. Use stable.
- E_i = 2 invfft? For weighted gradient: h = convolution(rev_p, w). E = 2 h[n-1:2n-1]. Need ensure nonnegative? w and p nonnegative so E≥0.
- λ = dot(p,E). Since E magnitude maybe ~? We can just use weighted mean. g = E - λ. This sum weighted zero: ∑p g=0. For normalized direction, scale = max(abs(g)) maybe. If scale too small, perturb.
- Step: start maybe 0.08. Multiplicative update p2=p*exp(-step*g/scale) (with g centered weighted). Exp may overflow if step*g/scale large up to step; if step=0.5 safe. Normalize. Then evaluate exact max (score). If smooth J decreases or exact max improves? Use line search: try steps [step, step/2, step/4,... maybe 6] choose one with best smooth J (or exact score if it improves). Update step based: if best at first increase step *1.08; else decrease. We can also use exact score for best but smooth for line search.
- Since objective nonconvex, use best-tracking and restarts/perturb.

Could be too slow: 2g per iter + exact b. But b computed already for w; exact max = max(b) after update requires one FFT. For line search multiple steps could multiply cost. We can update once and evaluate. Use adaptive step without many line searches? Could try 2-3 steps. Iterate count: n=1024, FFT size b 2048, g 8192 maybe. One iteration ~ maybe 0.005s? We can do 100k iter. Need tune.

Maybe use smaller n=512 for many starts, then evolve to 1024. Or optimize final at n=256 to 1.5? Target maybe 1.5027 and previous length 256, so n=256 could be enough. But larger can approach continuous. We'll allocate time: first refine given n=256 to see if improve; then upscale to 1024 and refine; final. But need robust total 2400.

Potential enhancements:
- Use exact active set with "top gap" weights. Instead of LSE, we can choose active set: let bmax=max(b); select indices where b > bmax - tau * bmax (e.g., tau=0.01 or 0.02) or top m = max(3, int(0.02*L)). Set weights = (bmax - b)^γ for selected, normalized. This avoids underflow and can enforce equioscillation. Could be faster and more focused. LSE may over-smooth. Could implement both: adaptive weights = softmax of top. Maybe use rank-based: active = indices with b >= bmax - 0.03*bmax, w=1/|active|. This is subgradient; gradient may jump but line search/perturb can handle. However selecting top may miss close ones; LSE smooth.

- Use "minorize maximize" (MM) update: For objective max b, we can upper bound max b by current M + sum weights(b-M) (linearization). Minimizing upper bound over p≥0 sum1 yields? Upper bound = M + ∑ w_k b_k - M∑w. The quadratic term ∑w b = p^T W_op p, where W_op = sum w_k convolution operators. Minimizing quadratic over simplex + quadratic term. If W_op positive semidefinite? The operator may be indefinite, but for nonnegative operators maybe? We can solve via multiplicative update: p_i ∝ 1/(diagonal? + offdiag?) Specifically minimize p^T H p subject p≥0 sum1; update p_i <- p_i / (H p)_i (multiplicative gradient descent). This is like our gradient E (H p) with weights. Indeed E=2Hp. So our multiplicative update p ∝ p exp(-η H p) approximates MM. Good.

- Could solve exact quadratic minimization for active weights: minimize M + p^T H p (plus linear) under simplex. H is symmetric? H from weighted convolution with w may be symmetric? The operator H_ji = ∑w_k? For ordinary convolution H = sum w_k C_k (shifts) may not be symmetric? But objective ∑w b = p^T H p, H maybe not symmetric if w not symmetric? For product p^T H p, only symmetric part matters; gradient 2S p. Our E =2S p with S symmetric part. We can use multiplicative update p <- p / (E + μ?) Could be standard.

Let's consider using entropy regularization: minimize F = maxb + γ H(p) (negative entropy?) to avoid zeros. Not necessary.

Maybe use "simulated annealing" with random multiplicative noise: occasionally multiply p by exp(noise) and accept if smooth/strict lower. This can escape local minima.

Function gradient_weights(p, w, fft_size, n):
    rp = p[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rp, fft_size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(R * W, fft_size)
    # length total >=3n-2; take indices n-1 : 2n-1
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
Double for E? Gradient E = 2*conv. Let's include factor 2.

- If beta <= 0 or bmax <= tiny, weights uniform? But sum1, b positive.

Optimization inner loop details:
```
def refine_mirror(p, deadline, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_max=500.0, step_start=0.25, beta_inc_steps=500):
    p=normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    L=2*n-1
    cfft=pow2(L)
    gfft=pow2(3*n-2)
    b=conv(p)
    maxb=max(b)
    score=2*n*maxb
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    beta=beta_start/maxb? 
```

Line search:
```
step = 0.35
fail=0
it=0
beta_target=15
while time<deadline:
  b=conv(p)
  maxb=np.max(b)
  beta=min(1e7, beta_target / maxb) # maxb >0
  w,lse_old = lse_weights(b,beta)
  E=2*gradient(p,w,gfft,n)
  lam=np.dot(p,E)
  g=E - lam

      b=conv(p);...

  best_step=step; best_obj=np.inf; best_p=None
  # maybe try multiple step factors [1,0.5,0.25,0.125]
  for s in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125]:
      u = -s * g / scale
      u = np.clip(u, -5, 5) # exp safe
      p2 = p * np.exp(u)
      p2 = sanitize_norm(p2)
      b2=conv(p2)
      maxb2=np.max(b2)
      # smooth objective with same beta? Need recompute lse? That's 2 extra FFT per candidate. Too costly.
      # Instead use exact score for accept? We can compute maxb2 (already from conv). For line search, exact max may be non-smooth but okay. If maxb2 < maxb - tol, accept. But for learning, use b2 max. If none improve, maybe still take step with best exact (least bad) if smooth? Need avoid oscillation. We can use objective = maxb2 (no lse). Since b2 computed, okay. Evaluate maxb2.
      if maxb2 < best_obj: best_obj=maxb2; best_p=p2
  # We need maybe allow no immediate max decrease if beta active set? Could use lse_new requires weights but expensive. Use exact; if accepted, improve fail. But if no decrease, reduce step.
  if best_p is not None and (best_obj < maxb or (best_obj <= maxb and fail large?)):
       p=best_p; maxb=best_obj; step=min(0.8, step*1.05); fail=0
  else:

```
def _lse_and_p(b, beta):
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1:
       return full(1/L), m
    x = beta*(b-m)
    x = np.clip(x, -700, 0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = e.sum()
    if s==0: e=max idx=1; s=1
    p = e/s
    val = m + np.log(s)/beta
    return p,val
```
Objective value scale maybe if beta large, val≈m. Fine.

```
def _sanitize_normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    a = np.nan_to_num(...)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    n=a.size
    if not finite or s<=1e-300: return uniform
    a=a/s
    if floor>0:
       a=np.maximum(a,floor)
       s=a.sum(); a=a/s
    return a
```

```
def _perturb_smooth(p, rng, strength=0.3):
    n=p.size
    if n<8: noise = rng.normal(size=n)
    else:
       z=rng.normal(size=n)
       F=np.fft.rfft(z)
       cutoff=max(2, min(len(F), max(4, n//32)))
       F[cutoff:]=0
       f=np.fft.irfft(F,n)
       f-=f.mean()
       sd=f.std(); if sd>0: f/=sd else uniform
    q = p * np.exp(strength*f)
    # maybe small uniform mixing
    q = 0.98*q + 0.02*(np.ones(n)/n)
    return normalize(q)
```

def _weights_from_b(b, beta_target):
   M=max(b)
   beta=beta_target/M
   w = softmax(beta*(b-M))
   # add small uniform: tau=0.05
   w = (1-tau)*w + tau/L
```

Now, refine function. Need be careful with time and return. Pseudocode:
```
def _refine_mirror(p, deadline, rng, beta0=20.0, beta1=300.0, step0=0.35, max_it? no):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n < 32: # too small maybe expand? We'll not call for tiny.
    csize = _pow2(2*n-1)
    gsize = _pow2(3*n-2)
    b = _conv2(p, csize)
    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    score=2*n*maxb
    bestp=p.copy(); bestscore=score
    beta_target=beta0
    step=step0
    fail=0
    it=0
    last_best_it=0
    b_of_cache? no
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        if maxb <= 0: ...
        beta = min(1e7, max(1.0, beta_target / maxb))
        w, lse_old = _lse_and_p(b, beta)
        # optional relax weights with topK? skip
        E = 2.0 * _convolve_rev_and_w(p, w, gsize, n)
        # E dimension n
        lam = float(np.dot(p, E))
        g = E - lam
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not finite or scale < 1e-15:
            p = _perturb(bestp, rng, 0.35)
            b = _conv2(p,csize); maxb=...; score=...
            step=step0; fail=0; beta_target=max(15.0, beta_target*0.5)
            continue
        # line search
        accepted=False
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
            s=step*mult
            if s <= 1e-6: break
            u = -s * g / scale
            # clip
            u = np.clip(u, -4.0, 4.0)
            p2 = p * np.exp(u)
            p2 = _sanitize(p2, 1e-12/n)
            if p2 is None: continue
            b2 = _conv2(p2, csize)
            maxb2 = float(np.max(b2))
            lse2 = _lse_value(b2, beta)
            # acceptance condition
            if lse2 < lse_old - 1e-13 and maxb2 <= maxb * 1.20:
                 p=p2; b=b2; maxb=maxb2; step=min(0.9, step*1.08); fail=0; accepted=True
                 if maxb < bestmax - 1e-14: bestp=p.copy(); bestmax=...; last_best_it=it
                 break
            # If lse not improve but exact improves? We can still accept if maxb2 < maxb and lse2 <= lse_old + 1e-10 maybe.
            elif maxb2 < maxb - 1e-14 and lse2 <= lse_old + 1e-10:
                 accept...
        if not accepted:
            step = max(0.02, step*0.5)
            fail += 1
            if fail > 80:
                 # try tiny random move? maybe accept best exact if current worsened? 
                 p = _perturb(bestp, rng, 0.25)
                 b = _conv2(p,csize); maxb=...; bestmax maybe update? score
                 step=0.35; fail=0; beta_target=max(beta0,beta_target*0.6)
        # beta schedule
        if it % 100 == 0:
            beta_target = min(beta1, beta_target * 1.05)  # or +
        if it - last_best_it > 1500 and it > 500 and time.monotonic() < deadline-10:
            # restart with perturbation, lower beta
            p = _perturb(bestp, rng, 0.4 if it>5000 else 0.25)
            b=...; maxb=...
            step=0.4; beta_target=max(beta0, beta_target*0.5)
            fail=0
    return bestp,bestscore
```

Weights with indices:
```
def _active_weights(b, beta, max_active):
    M = float(np.max(b))
    # LSE value full
    val = _lse_value(b,beta)
    # Gap threshold: keep b where exp(beta(b-M)) > 1e-6
    gap = 14.0 / beta  # since log 1e-6
    mask = b >= M - gap
    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
       idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    max_active = max(1, int(max_active))
    if idx.size > max_active:
       # select top max_active using argpartition
       part = np.argpartition(b, -max_active)[-max_active:]
       idx = np.sort(part)
    elif idx.size < 5:
       K = max(5, idx.size)
       if K <= b.size:
           part = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
           idx = part # no need sort
    w = np.exp(beta*(b[idx]-M))
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w=np.ones(idx.size)/idx.size
    else: w=w/s
    return idx,w,val
```
`gap=14/beta` active where weight >e^-14 ~8e-7. max_active maybe 80 or 160. If active broad and max_active cap, we drop small weights; maybe okay. Function `_active_weights_and_lse(b, beta_target)`:
```
def _active_weights_and_lse(b, beta_target, max_active):
    M = float(np.max(b))
    beta = max(1.0, min(1e7, beta_target / max(M,1e-300)))
    val = _lse_value(b,beta)
    if b.size ==0: return np.zeros(0), np.zeros(0), M, beta, val
    # gap based on 12 temp
    gap = 12.0 / beta
    # if gap > M? then all; else threshold
    thr = M - gap
    idx = np.nonzero(b >= thr)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    else:
        if idx.size > max_active:
            # select top max_active
            # np.argpartition(b, -max_active)[-max_active:]
            part = np.argpartition(b, -max_active)[-max_active:]
            idx = part
        elif idx.size < 8:
            K = min(b.size, max(8, int(max_active*0.1)))
            part = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
            idx = part
    # weights among idx
    bx = b.astype(float)
    arg = beta*(bx[idx] - M)
    arg = np.clip(arg, -745.0, 0.0)
    weights = np.exp(arg)
    s = weights.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
        weights = np.ones(idx.size)/max(1,idx.size)
    else:
        weights = weights / s
    # maybe add small uniform to selected weights to avoid one active:
    # weights = (1-0.05)*weights + 0.05/idx.size
    return idx, weights, M, beta, val
```
max_active passed. If idx size maybe unsorted, okay.
For b with small negative due FFT, max positive. If b all zero? sum p=1 so not.

Threshold 12/beta: If beta=10000, gap=0.0012; M=0.0007 gap >M, all active -> cap max_active. If beta smaller, active broad. Good. max_active maybe n//4? For n=1024, 256. If all active, select top256.

LSE value with beta maybe stable:
```
def _lse_value(b,beta):
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if beta <=1: return m
    x=beta*(b-m); x=np.clip(x,-745,0); s=np.exp(x).sum(); if s<=0 return m; return m+np.log(s)/beta
```
If beta huge and multiple max, log count /beta small.

Param start list generation. To ensure variety, random states. Let's define:
```
def _param_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(_make_uniform(n))
    for a in (-1.0,-0.6,-0.3,0.3,0.6,1.0): starts.append(_make_linear(n,a))
    for alpha,beta in [(1,0),(0,1),(2,0),(0,2),(1,1),(0.5,1.5),(1.5,0.5)]: starts.append(_make_power(n,alpha,beta))
    for m,a in [(1,0.5),(1,0.8),(3,0.5),(3,0.8),(5,0.5),(5,0.8)]: starts.append(_make_cheb(n,m,a))
    for m,a in [(1,1.0),(3,1.0),(5,1.0)]: starts.append(_make_logcheb(n,m,a))
    # gauss mixtures
    starts.append(_make_gauss_mix(n,(0.25,0.75),0.18))
    starts.append(_make_gauss_mix(n,(0.2,0.8),0.12))
    starts.append(_make_gauss_mix(n,(0.3,0.7),0.25))
    # random smooth
    starts.append(_make_smooth_random(n,rng,base=_make_uniform(n),amplitude=0.7))
    starts.append(_make_smooth_random(n,rng,base=_make_linear(n,0.6),amplitude=0.5))
    return starts
```

def _refine_mirror(p, deadline, rng, max_active=None, beta0=10.0, beta1=120.0, step0=0.40):
    p=_normalize(p); n=p.size
    if n<16: ...
    if max_active is None: max_active = min(256, max(32, n//4))
    csize=_pow2(2*n-1)
    floor=1e-12/n
    b=_conv_sq(p,csize); maxb=...; best...
    beta_target=beta0; step=step0; fail=0; it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       idx, w, M, beta, lse_old = _active_weights_and_lse(b,beta_target,max_active)
       E=2*_gradient_active(p, idx, w)
       lam=float(np.dot(p,E))
       g=E-lam
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if not finite or scale<1e-15:
           p=_perturb(bestp,rng,0.4)
           b=...; maxb=...; step=step0; fail=0; beta_target=max(beta0,beta_target*0.5); continue
       best_candidate=None; best_lse=np.inf; best_max=np.inf; best_p=None
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
           s=step*mult
           if s<=1e-4: break
           u=-s*g/scale
           u=np.clip(u,-4,4)
           p2=p*np.exp(u)
           p2=_sanitize(p2,floor)
           b2=_conv_sq(p2,csize)
           maxb2=float(np.max(b2))
           lse2=_lse_value(b2,beta)
           if (lse2 < best_lse - 1e-15 or (abs(lse2-best_lse)<1e-15 and maxb2<best_max)):
               best_lse=lse2; best_max=maxb2; best_p=p2; best_b=b2
           if lse2 < lse_old - 1e-13 and maxb2 <= maxb*1.15:
               p=p2; b=b2; maxb=maxb2; ...
               break
       else:
           # no accepted; if after 50 fails, maybe accept best candidate if it doesn't hurt exact much to progress
           if fail > 40 and best_lse < lse_old + 1e-11 and best_max <= maxb*1.02:
               p=best_p; b=best_b; maxb=best_max; step=min(0.9, step*1.02); fail=0
           else:
               step=max(0.03, step*0.5); fail+=1
               if fail > 120:
                   p=_perturb(bestp,rng,0.35)
                   b=...; maxb=...; step=step0; fail=0; beta_target=max(beta0, beta_target*0.5)
       # update beta
       if it % 200 ==0:
           beta_target=min(beta1, beta_target*1.05)
       if score<best... update
       if it % 10 ==0 maybe print? Avoid too many prints; every 1000 maybe.
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(n):
    if n<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()
```
For n=0? return1.

`_normalize(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
def _normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0:
       return np.ones(1)
    if floor>0:
       a=np.maximum(a,float(floor))
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    return a/s
```

`_conv_sq(a, csize)`:
```
def _conv_sq(a,csize):
    n=a.size
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,csize)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,csize)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
Irfft returns length csize; if csize odd? pow2 even; okay. If tiny negative, leave.

`_lse_value(b,beta)` as above.

`_active_weights_and_lse(b,beta_target,max_active)`:
Need max_active >=1. b length L. If beta_target/M, M maybe 0. If M=0, return idx [0], [1]. Use M = max(float(np.max(b)), 1e-300).
```
def _active_weights_and_lse(b, beta_target, max_active):
    L=b.size
    M=float(np.max(b))
    if M<=0: M=1e-300
    beta=float(beta_target)/M
    if beta<1: beta=1.0
    if beta>1e7: beta=1e7
    lse=_lse_value(b,beta)
    if L==0: ...
    max_active=max(1,min(int(max_active), L))
    gap=12.0/beta
    thr=M-gap
    if thr <= 0:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.nonzero(b>=thr)[0]
    if idx.size==0:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    elif idx.size > max_active:
       part=np.argpartition(b, -max_active)[-max_active:]
       idx=part
    elif idx.size < 8:
       K=min(L, max(8, int(round(0.25*max_active))))
       if K > idx.size:
          part=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
          idx=part
    bx=b[idx]
    x=beta*(bx-M)
    x=np.clip(x,-745,0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=float(np.sum(w))
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.full(idx.size,1.0/idx.size)
    else:
       w=w/s
    # optional blend uniform 0.05
    if idx.size>1:
       w=(1-0.05)*w + 0.05/idx.size
    return idx,w,M,beta,lse
```
Blending uniform avoids too sharp. But if max_active large, uniform part okay.

If b shape negative, M positive. idx argpartition okay.

`_gradient_active(p, idx, w)`:
```
def _gradient_active(p, idx, w):
    n=p.size
    g=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    for ki in range(idx.size):
       wi=float(w[ki])
       if wi<=1e-14: continue
       k=int(idx[ki])
       lo = k - (n-1)
       if lo < 0: lo = 0
       hi = k
       if hi > n-1: hi = n-1
       if lo <= hi:
           start_p = k - hi
           end_p = k - lo
           # ensure bounds
           if start_p <0: start_p=0
           if end_p >= n: end_p=n-1
           seg=p[start_p:end_p+1][::-1]
           g[lo:hi+1] += wi*seg
    return g
```
Need be careful if k > 2n-2? idx length 2n-1 so k valid. start_p/end_p within 0..n-1? If k maybe 2n-2, hi=n-1, lo=n-1, start_p=k-hi=n-1, end_p=k-lo=n-1 good. If k=0 start=0 end=0.

Return gradient E = 2*g.

`_sanitize(p,floor)`:
```
def _sanitize(a, floor):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   if floor>0: a=np.maximum(a,floor)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return None
   return a/s
```

Perturb:
```
def _perturb(p, rng, strength=0.3):
   n=p.size
   if n<16:
       noise=rng.normal(size=n)
   else:
       z=rng.normal(size=n)
       F=np.fft.rfft(z)
       cutoff=max(3, min(len(F), n//12 + 2))
       if cutoff < len(F): F[cutoff:]=0.0
       f=np.fft.irfft(F,n).real
       f=f-f.mean()
       sd=float(f.std())
       if sd>1e-15: f=f/sd
       else: f=np.zeros(n)
   q=p*np.exp(strength*f)
   # tiny random floor
   q=q + 1e-6 * (rng.random(n) + 0.5*n? ) Actually add small uniform:
   q = q + (1e-8 / n) * rng.random(n)
   return _normalize(q)
```
Maybe `q + 1e-8 * (np.ones(n)/n)` not random. Use `1e-8/n * rng.random(n)` adds sum ~5e-10, tiny.

Param starts:
```
def _make_uniform(n): return np.ones(max(2,n), dtype=float)/max(2,n)
```

`_make_odd(n, mode,a)`:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n); u=2*x-1
h=np.zeros(n)
if mode==0: h=u
elif mode==1: base=u*(1-u*u); if maxabs>0: h=base/maxabs
elif mode==2: base=u*(1-u**4); h=base/maxabs
elif mode==3: h=4*u**3-3*u
elif mode==4: base=(4*u**3-3*u)*(1-u*u); h=base/maxabs
if a is None: return normalize(max(h,0)? no h can negative)
p=1.0 + float(a)*h
return _normalize(np.maximum(p,0.0))
```

`_make_gauss_mix(n, centers, width)`:

```

    if seq is None: return None

    except: return None

    if arr.size==0: return None
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return None
    return arr/s
```

Let's design `_lse_weights_full(b,beta)` returns all weights and value. Same as _lse_and_p. Then gradient via FFT:
```
def _gradient_fft(p,w,gsize,n):
   rp=p[::-1]
   R=np.fft.rfft(rp,gsize)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,gsize)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(R*W,gsize).real
   return 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

```
def _odd_poly_value(n, mode):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); u=2*x-1; h=np.zeros(n)
    if mode==0: h=u
    elif mode==1: h=u*(1-u*u)
    elif mode==2: h=u*(1-u**4)
    elif mode==3: h=4*u**3-3*u
    elif mode==4: h=(4*u**3-3*u)*(1-u*u)
    else: h=u
    m=np.max(np.abs(h))
    if m>0: h=h/m
    return h
```
For mode3 Chebyshev max1; normalizing okay. For mode0 max1. mode4 max? normalized. Good.

```
def _make_smooth_random(n, rng, base=None, amplitude=0.6):
   n=max(int(n),2)
   if base is None or base.size != n: base=_make_uniform(n)
   z=rng.normal(size=n)
   F=np.fft.rfft(z)
   cutoff=max(2, min(len(F), max(3, n//12 +2)))
   F[cutoff:]=0.0
   f=np.fft.irfft(F,n).real
   f=f-f.mean()
   sd=float(f.std())
   if sd>1e-15: f=f/sd
   else: f=np.zeros(n)
   p=base*(1.0+float(amplitude)*f)
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   return _normalize(p,1e-14/n)
```

Good.

x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
return _normalize(new)
```

```
def _lse_and_p(b,beta):
    L=b.size
    if L==0: return np.zeros(1),0
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
       return np.full(L,1.0/L),m
    x=beta*(b-m)
    x=np.clip(x,-745.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s<=0 or not finite: e=np.zeros(L); e[argmax]=1; s=1
    p=e/s
    val=m + np.log(s)/beta
    return p,val
```
Use `np.argmax`.

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative self-convolutions.

The score is
    score(a) = 2 * n * max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2 .
It is scale invariant, so all sequences are normalized to sum 1.

This implementation uses:
  * multi-start initialization from analytic families, smooth random starts,
    and the previously found sequence;
  * a smooth log-sum-exp surrogate for max(a*a);
  * mirror-descent on the positive simplex with FFT-weighted gradients
    and backtracking line search;
  * periodic annealing/beta sharpness schedule and restarts;
  * optional final upsampling and zero-threshold polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(n):
    """Smallest power of two >= n (at least 1)."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    """Raw nonnegative vector to a nonnegative vector with sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _conv_sq(a, fft_size):
    """Linear convolution a*a by zero-padded real FFT."""
    n = int(a.size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_size).real
    return b[:2 * n - 1]


def _score_array(a):
    """Fast approximate score using FFT, identical to evaluator up to roundoff."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    fft_size = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    b = _conv_sq(a, fft_size)
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mb


def _lse_value(b, beta):
    """Stable log-sum-exp of b / beta, with offset at max(b)."""
    if b.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
        return m
    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return float(m + np.log(s) / beta)


def _lse_and_weights(b, beta):
    """
    Soft weights proportional to exp(beta*(b-max)) and the LSE value.
    The weights sum to 1.
    """
    L = int(b.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(1), 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64), m

    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0

    w = e / s
    val = float(m + np.log(s) / beta)
    return w, val


def _gradient_fft(p, w, fft_size, n):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (p*p)_k w.r.t. p_i:
        g_i = 2 * sum_k w_k p_{k-i}.
    Computed by FFT convolution with reversed p.
    """
    rp = p[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rp, fft_size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(R * W, fft_size).real
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _sanitize_update(a, floor):
    """Clip to nonnegative, enforce tiny floor, normalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _perturb(p, rng, strength=0.30):
    """Smooth multiplicative perturbation, with a small uniform reviver."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n < 16:
        f = rng.normal(size=n)
    else:
        z = rng.normal(size=n)
        F = np.fft.rfft(z)
        cutoff = max(3, min(len(F), max(4, n // 12 + 2)))
        if cutoff < len(F):
            F[cutoff:] = 0.0
        f = np.fft.irfft(F, n).real
        f -= float(np.mean(f))
        sd = float(np.std(f))
        if sd > 1e-15:
            f = f / sd
        else:
            f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = _normalize(p)
    if rng.random() < 0.10:
        p = p[::-1].copy()

    q = p * np.exp(float(strength) * f)
    q = q + (1e-8 / float(n)) * (1.0 + rng.random(n))
    return _normalize(q, 1e-14 / float(n))


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _make_linear(n, a):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = 1.0 + float(a) * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    return _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))


def _make_power(n, alpha, beta):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    eps = 0.5 / float(n)
    x = np.linspace(eps, 1.0 - eps, n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.power(x, float(alpha)) * np.power(1.0 - x, float(beta))
    return _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))


def _odd_poly_value(n, mode):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    if mode == 0:
        h = u
    elif mode == 1:
        h = u * (1.0 - u * u)
    elif mode == 2:
        h = u * (1.0 - u ** 4)
    elif mode == 3:
        h = 4.0 * u ** 3 - 3.0 * u
    elif mode == 4:
        h = (4.0 * u ** 3 - 3.0 * u) * (1.0 - u * u)
    else:
        h = u

    m = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if m > 0.0:
        h = h / m
    return h


def _make_odd_poly(n, mode, a):
    h = _odd_poly_value(n, mode)
    p = 1.0 + float(a) * h
    return _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))


def _make_log_odd_poly(n, mode, a):
    h = _odd_poly_value(n, mode)
    p = np.exp(float(a) * h)
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_sigmoid_odd(n, c, a):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    h = np.tanh(float(c) * u)
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if m > 0.0:
        h = h / m
    p = 1.0 + float(a) * h
    return _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))


def _make_trig_odd(n, m, a):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    # m should be odd, giving h(1-x) = -h(x).
    h = np.cos(np.pi * float(m) * x)
    p = 1.0 + float(a) * h
    return _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))


def _make_gauss_mix(n, centers, width):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = float(width)
    cent = tuple(float(c) for c in centers)
    for c in cent:
        p += np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / w) ** 2)
    return _normalize(np.maximum(p, 0.0))


def _make_smooth_random(n, rng, base=None, amplitude=0.6):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    if base is None or base.size != n:
        base = _make_uniform(n)
    base = _normalize(base)

    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cutoff = max(2, min(len(F), max(3, n // 12 + 2)))
    if cutoff < len(F):
        F[cutoff:] = 0.0
    f = np.fft.irfft(F, n).real
    f -= float(np.mean(f))
    sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd > 1e-15:
        f = f / sd
    else:
        f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = base * (1.0 + float(amplitude) * f)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return _normalize(p, 1e-14 / float(n))


def _resample_sequence(old, new_n):
    old = _normalize(old)
    new_n = max(2, int(new_n))
    if old.size == 0:
        return _make_uniform(new_n)
    if old.size == new_n:
        return old.copy()
    if old.size == 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return _normalize(new)


def _signature(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    step = max(1, p.size // 128)
    vals = np.round(p[::step], 12)
    return tuple(float(x) for x in vals)


def _add_unique(out, seen, a):
    if a is None:
        return
    a = _normalize(a)
    sig = _signature(a)
    if sig not in seen:
        seen.add(sig)
        out.append(a)


def _param_candidates(n):
    """Generate a broad pool of low-dimensional heuristic starts."""
    n = max(32, int(n))
    out = []

    out.append(_make_uniform(n))

    for a in (-1.0, -0.7, -0.4, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0):
        out.append(_make_linear(n, a))

    powers = [
        (1.0, 0.0), (0.0, 1.0),
        (2.0, 0.0), (0.0, 2.0),
        (1.0, 1.0),
        (0.5, 1.5), (1.5, 0.5),
        (0.25, 0.25),
    ]
    for alpha, beta in powers:
        out.append(_make_power(n, alpha, beta))

    odd_modes = [
        (0, 0.5), (0, 0.8),
        (1, 0.8), (2, 0.8),
        (3, 0.8), (4, 0.8),
        (1, 1.0), (3, 1.0), (4, 1.0),
    ]
    for mode, a in odd_modes:
        out.append(_make_odd_poly(n, mode, a))

    log_odd_modes = [
        (0, 1.0), (1, 1.5), (2, 2.0), (3, 2.0),
    ]
    for mode, a in log_odd_modes:
        out.append(_make_log_odd_poly(n, mode, a))

    sig_terms = [
        (3.0, 0.7), (6.0, 0.9), (12.0, 1.0), (20.0, 1.0),
        (6.0, 0.5), (12.0, 0.7),
    ]
    for c, a in sig_terms:
        out.append(_make_sigmoid_odd(n, c, a))

    trig_terms = [
        (1, 0.6), (3, 0.9), (5, 0.9), (7, 1.0), (9, 1.0), (15, 1.0),
        (1, 0.9), (5, 0.6), (11, 1.0),
    ]
    for m, a in trig_terms:
        if m % 2 == 1:
            out.append(_make_trig_odd(n, m, a))

    out.append(_make_gauss_mix(n, (0.25, 0.75), 0.18))
    out.append(_make_gauss_mix(n, (0.25, 0.75), 0.10))
    out.append(_make_gauss_mix(n, (0.20, 0.80), 0.12))
    out.append(_make_gauss_mix(n, (0.30, 0.70), 0.22))
    out.append(_make_gauss_mix(n, (0.15, 0.85), 0.16))

    out.append(_make_smooth_random(n, np.random.default_rng(0xC0FFEE), base=_make_uniform(n), amplitude=0.8))
    out.append(_make_smooth_random(n, np.random.default_rng(0xBEEF01), base=_make_linear(n, 0.7), amplitude=0.5))
    out.append(_make_smooth_random(n, np.random.default_rng(0xBEEF02), base=_make_odd_poly(n, 1, 0.9), amplitude=0.5))
    out.append(_make_smooth_random(n, np.random.default_rng(0xBEEF03), base=_make_sigmoid_odd(n, 12.0, 1.0), amplitude=0.45))

    return out


def _make_starts(n, given, best, rng, count):
    """Assemble candidate initial sequences for a fixed length."""
    n = max(32, int(n))
    count = max(1, int(count))
    out = []
    seen = set()

    if given is not None and given.size > 4:
        _add_unique(out, seen, _resample_sequence(given, n))
        _add_unique(out, seen, _resample_sequence(given[::-1], n))

    if best is not None and best.size > 4:
        _add_unique(out, seen, _resample_sequence(best, n))
        _add_unique(out, seen, _resample_sequence(best[::-1], n))

        if len(out) < count:
            base_best = _resample_sequence(best, n)
            mixed = 0.85 * base_best + 0.15 * _make_smooth_random(
                n, rng, base=base_best, amplitude=0.7
            )
            _add_unique(out, seen, mixed)

    if len(out) < count:
        params = _param_candidates(n)
        scored = []
        counter = 0
        for arr in params:
            s = float(_score_array(arr))
            if np.isfinite(s):
                scored.append((s, counter, arr))
                counter += 1
        scored.sort()

        # Prefer stronger heuristic starts, but keep some diversity.
        for _, _, arr in scored:
            _add_unique(out, seen, arr)
            if len(out) >= count:
                break

    # Last resorts.
    while len(out) < count:
        _add_unique(out, seen, _make_smooth_random(n, rng, base=_make_uniform(n), amplitude=1.0))
        _add_unique(out, seen, _make_linear(n, float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))))
        _add_unique(out, seen, _make_odd_poly(n, int(rng.integers(0, 5)), float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0))))

    return out[:count]


def _refine_mirror(p, deadline, rng, beta0=12.0, beta1=140.0, step0=0.35):
    """
    Mirror-descent refinement on the positive simplex.

    Minimizes an LSE surrogate of max(a*a).  Returns the best exact-max point found.
    """
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 32:
        n = 32
        p = _make_uniform(n)

    csize = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    gsize = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    floor = 1e-12 / float(n)

    b = _conv_sq(p, csize)
    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    best_maxb = maxb
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_maxb

    beta_target = float(beta0)
    step = float(step0)
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last_exact_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if maxb <= 1e-300:
            maxb = 1e-300

        beta = max(1.0, min(1e7, float(beta_target) / maxb))

        w, lse_old = _lse_and_weights(b, beta)
        g = _gradient_fft(p, w, gsize, n)

        # Project gradient to sum-zero tangent: subtract weighted mean.
        lam = float(np.dot(p, g))
        g = g - lam
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))

        if (not np.isfinite(scale)) or scale <= 1e-14:
            p = _perturb(best_p, rng, 0.35)
            b = _conv_sq(p, csize)
            maxb = float(np.max(b))
            step = float(step0)
            fail = 0
            beta_target = max(float(beta0), beta_target * 0.5)
            continue

        accepted = False
        best_candidate_p = None
        best_candidate_b = None
        best_candidate_max = np.inf
        best_candidate_lse = np.inf

        mults = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25) if step > 0.12 else (1.0, 0.5)
        for mult in mults:
            s = step * mult
            if s <= 1e-6:
                break

            u = -s * g / scale
            u = np.clip(u, -4.0, 4.0)
            q = p * np.exp(u)
            q = _sanitize_update(q, floor)
            if q is None:
                continue

            b2 = _conv_sq(q, csize)
            maxb2 = float(np.max(b2))
            lse2 = _lse_value(b2, beta)

            if (not np.isfinite(maxb2)) or (not np.isfinite(lse2)):
                continue

            if (lse2 < best_candidate_lse - 1e-15) or \
               (lse2 <= best_candidate_lse + 1e-15 and maxb2 < best_candidate_max):
                best_candidate_p = q
                best_candidate_b = b2
                best_candidate_max = maxb2
                best_candidate_lse = lse2

            # Accept if smooth surrogate improves, or exact max improves nearly monotonically.
            if (lse2 < lse_old - 1e-13 and maxb2 <= maxb * 1.15) or \
               (maxb2 < maxb - 1e-14 and lse2 <= lse_old + 1e-10):
                p = q
                b = b2
                maxb = maxb2
                step = min(0.9, step * 1.04)
                fail = 0
                accepted = True

                if maxb < best_maxb - 1e-14:
                    best_maxb = maxb
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_maxb
                    last_exact_improve = it
                break

        if not accepted:
            # If very stuck, allow a tiny-worse neighborhood move to escape.
            if best_candidate_p is not None and fail > 30 and \
               best_candidate_lse < lse_old + 1e-12 and \
               best_candidate_max <= maxb * 1.02:
                p = best_candidate_p
                b = best_candidate_b
                maxb = best_candidate_max
                step = min(0.9, step * 1.01)
                fail = 0
                accepted = True

                if maxb < best_maxb - 1e-14:
                    best_maxb = maxb
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_maxb
                    last_exact_improve = it
            else:
                step = max(0.03, step * 0.5)
                fail += 1

        if accepted:
            # An increase in beta sharpens the active set; do it slowly.
            if it % 150 == 0:
                beta_target = min(float(beta1), beta_target * 1.045)
        else:
            if fail > 120:
                p = _perturb(best_p, rng, 0.38)
                b = _conv_sq(p, csize)
                maxb = float(np.max(b))
                step = float(step0)
                fail = 0
                beta_target = max(float(beta0), beta_target * 0.55)

        # Coarse restart if exact optimum is not moving.
        if it - last_exact_improve > 1400 and it > 500 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
            p = _perturb(best_p, rng, 0.42 if it > 12000 else 0.30)
            b = _conv_sq(p, csize)
            maxb = float(np.max(b))
            step = float(step0)
            fail = 0
            beta_target = max(float(beta0), beta_target * 0.45)

        if it % 2500 == 0:
            print(
                f"refine n={n} it={it} score={2.0*n*maxb:.9f} "
                f"best={best_score:.9f} betaT={beta_target:.2f}",
                flush=True,
            )

    return best_p, best_score


def _get_given_sequence():
    """Fetch the optional previous best global sequence, if present."""
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all():
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Runs until the deadline and returns the best
    nonnegative coefficient list found.
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(budget, 2400.0)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    margin = max(25.0, 0.02 * budget)
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget) - margin
    scale = max(0.35, min(1.0, budget / 2400.0))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    given = _get_given_sequence()

    if given is not None:
        best_p = _normalize(given)
        best_score = float(_score_array(best_p))
    else:
        best_p = _make_uniform(512)
        best_score = 2.0

    print(f"initial best n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Staged search.  Smaller lengths are fast; good shapes later get resampled.
    stage_configs = [
        # length, number of starts, nominal seconds per start
        (256, 8, 50),
        (512, 6, 60),
        (1024, 6, 90),
        (2048, 3, 120),
    ]

    for n, count, sec in stage_configs:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 60.0:
            break

        sec = max(15, int(sec * scale))
        starts = _make_starts(int(n), given, best_p, rng, count)
        print(f"stage n={n} starts={len(starts)} score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

        for idx, init in enumerate(starts):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0
            if rem < 12.0:
                break

            init = _normalize(init)
            init_score = float(_score_array(init))
            if not np.isfinite(init_score):
                continue

            alloc = min(sec, rem)
            if init_score > best_score + 0.04:
                alloc = min(alloc, 0.65 * sec)
            if alloc < 8.0:
                break

            t_end = time.monotonic() + alloc
            candidate, cand_score = _refine_mirror(
                init.copy(), t_end, rng,
                beta0=10.0, beta1=130.0, step0=0.38
            )
            cand_score = float(cand_score)

            print(
                f"n={candidate.size} start={idx}/{len(starts)} init={init_score:.8f} "
                f"new={cand_score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f}",
                flush=True,
            )

            if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p = np.asarray(candidate, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                best_score = cand_score

    # If current best is still weak, try to move it to a larger length.
    if best_score > 1.535 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 160.0:
        cur_n = int(best_p.size)
        target_n = max(1024, min(2048, 2 * cur_n if cur_n > 0 else 1024))
        if target_n > cur_n:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
            if rem > 80.0:
                t_end = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, min(200.0, rem * 0.9)))
                init = _resample_sequence(best_p, target_n)
                init = 0.92 * init + 0.08 * _make_odd_poly(target_n, 1, 0.8)
                cand, cand_score = _refine_mirror(init, t_end, rng, beta0=14.0, beta1=170.0, step0=0.32)
                cand_score = float(cand_score)
                print(f"upscale {cur_n}->{cand.size} score={cand_score:.9f}", flush=True)
                if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_p = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    best_score = cand_score

    # Final deep refinement of the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 4.0
        if rem > 25.0:
            cand, cand_score = _refine_mirror(
                best_p.copy(), deadline - 3.0, rng,
                beta0=max(28.0, best_score * 10.0),
                beta1=max(260.0, best_score * 40.0),
                step0=0.22
            )
            cand_score = float(cand_score)
            print(f"final refine score={cand_score:.9f} best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                best_score = cand_score

    # Polish by trying a few zero thresholds.  Often a few exactly-zero entries help.
    final_p = _normalize(best_p)
    final_score = float(_score_array(final_p))

    for thr in (0.0, 1e-12, 1e-10, 1e-8, 1e-6, 1e-4, 1e-2):
        q = final_p.copy()
        if thr > 0.0:
            q[np.less(q, thr)] = 0.0
        q = _normalize(q)
        s = float(_score_array(q))
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < final_score - 1e-13:
            final_score = s
            final_p = q

    # Optional exact validation using the provided evaluator.
    try:
        ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_p])
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            if ev < final_score:
                final_score = float(ev)
        else:
            final_p = _make_uniform(512)
            final_score = 2.0
    except Exception:
        pass

    final_p = _normalize(final_p)
    final_p = np.nan_to_num(final_p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    final_p = np.maximum(final_p, 0.0)

    print(f"final score={final_score:.9f} length={final_p.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```
